You have also facilitated the opening of Govinda’s restaurants all over the world so that the ordinary person can enjoy *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*. So, following in your footsteps, we are striving to spread Lord Caitanya’s message in South London. There is no way we can repay our debt to you. Please accept whatever small insignificant service we are attempting to perform.

*Your servants in South London, UK.*  
(written by Kṛṣṇa Prema dāsa)

## Madrid

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you!

*tulayāma lavenāpi na svargaṁ nāpunar-bhavam*  
*bhagavat-saṅgi-saṅgasya martyānāṁ kim utāśiṣaḥ*

**“The value of a moment’s association with the devotee of the Lord cannot even be compared to the attainment of heavenly planets or liberation from matter, and what to speak of worldly benedictions in the form of material prosperity, which are for those who are meant for death.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.18.13)**

For us who are loitering in the middle of this terrible ocean of material existence, your traveling from Vṛndāvana to the West and your powerful and matchless preaching of Kṛṣṇa consciousness form the foundation and the essence of our lives.

It is said the biggest glory of the Supreme Personality of Godhead is His unlimited mercy to the fallen souls. This is also true about you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Certainly, most of our lives before knowing you were full of suffering and confusion, or at best were ordinary and empty. And our destiny was even worse. We were condemned by the all-powerful material energy of the Lord in this powerful and miserable Age of Kali. But you sacrificed everything to reach us and changed us completely. You didn’t act like the so-called “spiritualists,” who only deal with the material conditions of life. You were fully Kṛṣṇa conscious, and therefore you never had the least doubt or fear. You always did as you spoke. In fact, you were, and still are, the miraculous shelter of countless bewildered and weak souls who are looking for better things in life.

Your activities and achievements broke all records: you spread the Holy Name everywhere, gave initiation to thousands of disciples, wrote dozens and dozens of transcendental books, established more than a hundred Kṛṣṇa conscious centers, traveled tirelessly all over the world to distribute your message, created a worldwide well-organized society to promote the practice of devotional service, spoke thousands of times to varied audiences about the glories of Kṛṣṇa and devotional service, took care of each and every detail of the spiritual life of your followers, etc. Your example is unique and brilliant and will be recorded in history forever.

As far as we are concerned, we just want to follow your instructions and please you with our sincere efforts to become Kṛṣṇa conscious, and to help others to do the same. Despite all the difficulties and changes that destiny produces, you are illuminating and will continue to illuminate the hearts of all those who have been blessed with the capacity to appreciate your wonderful transcendental personality.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please forgive our weaknesses and mistakes and overlook our offenses. On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we pray only that you keep us engaged eternally in the loving service of the Supreme Lord and that you don’t allow us to forget the matchless gifts you have given us.

Aspiring for your merciful blessing,

*Your servants who meet at the ISKCON temple in Madrid, Spain.*

## Manipur

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We are the most fallen servants of your servants. On this most auspicious day of your divine appearance we do not have enough words to sing your divine glories. We humbly pray for your divine blessings so that we can offer a few words to your lotus feet.

During your sojourn on this earthly planet you planted a spiritual tree of *kṛṣṇa-prema—*ISKCON having an unlimited number of branches. Now the tree you planted some forty years ago has grown up fully to give its fruits in the form of effective preaching for *kṛṣṇa-prema* benefiting the whole world. This is the most wonderful gift that you gave to humanity.

Your Divine Grace, although your physical presence is no longer in our midst, you are always with your devotees, speaking through your books, demonstrating your presence through your most beloved disciples, distributing *kṛṣṇa-prema* to all the fallen souls through your *saṅkīrtana*, accompanied by your wonderful devotional voice.

In this Age of Kali, the age of quarrel and distrust, it is a known fact that this world is becoming a desert day by day, devoid of spiritual water, making it impossible for mankind to live peacefully. Your divine teachings are the real oasis for all the thirsty souls searching for the real purpose of life. You are so merciful to one and all.

On this most auspicious day we humbly pray for your unlimited blessings so that we can continue to serve your divine mission.

All glories to Your Divine Grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your most fallen servants at ISKCON Manipur, India.*  
(written by Pārtha Sārathi dāsa)

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

You came to this world as Lord Caitanya’s pure representative just to give everyone an opportunity to become His pure devotee. If any one of us wants to become a pure devotee of the Lord, there are no material conditions that can prevent us from succeeding. You established the International Society for Krishna Consciousness (ISKCON) to create an atmosphere that would allow everyone to associate with devotees and become Kṛṣṇa conscious. You knew that many things would change in our lives, in ISKCON, and in the world by the influence of the time factor. But you also understood that nothing could ever prevent any one of us from becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious if we sincerely wanted to become pure.

You adopted one slogan, “Utility is the principle,” which expresses the philosophical principle of *yukta-vairāgya,* or the proper utilization of everything in the service of the Lord. Based on that principle, we need to utilize everything in the Lord’s service—even adverse conditions. We too often try to blame our own spiritual shortcomings on problems in our personal lives, in the association of devotees, or in ISKCON. Some of these shortcomings are to neglect our vows to chant sixteen rounds of *japa* or to follow the four regulative principles. We may even leave your glorious ISKCON Society for another spiritual society, or give up our spiritual pursuit altogether.

But, if we look within our heart of hearts we will realize that our spiritual shortcomings are due to our own causeless unwillingness to surrender to the Lord, and that we are not innocent victims of any external cause. The fact is that whatever difficulties or disturbances we may experience are personally given to us by the Lord to help us advance. It is our responsibility as your followers to try to understand how to utilize every situation, including difficulties, to advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, to be connected to ISKCON is most auspicious. It has been said that you built a house in which the whole world could live. That house is the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. But to take shelter in the ISKCON house does not mean that everyone must live in one of your temples. This house is not bound by the physical walls of ordinary buildings. It only requires that one has some faith in you as the Lord’s pure representative. Anyone who has even a little faith in you as the Lord’s pure representative can take shelter in your house, no matter where he is or what his superficial condition. The Lord is supremely personal, and He reciprocates with each of us perfectly as we surrender to Him.

One may argue that ISKCON was previously very auspicious but has lost its auspiciousness since you completed your earthly pastimes in 1977. One may argue that we have destroyed your great Society and that what exists now in the name of ISKCON is something else. There is no doubt that our Society has many problems now, but it did also during your physical presence. You personally and expertly dealt with many, many problems that most of your followers were unaware of. In fact, every religious movement has had to overcome many obstacles. Compared to others, we are overcoming our problems at lightning speed by your mercy.

Many things have happened within our Society that have embarrassed us and you, and which have caused trouble to innumerable devotees. We are not proud of these incidents, but we also know that you will continue to guide us as our loving father and purify us individually and as a spiritual Society. You are helping to mold ISKCON into a super-potent spiritual Society that will guide the innocent victims of Kali-yuga. As long as we maintain faith in Your Divine Grace, you will continue to guide us and help us learn from our mistakes. For your sincere followers, you are as much with us now as you were during your physical presence.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray at your lotus feet for the strength to remain within your ISKCON Society. We pray that we may always see your hand and the hand of the Lord within our lives, and that we may use everything, including adverse conditions and our mistakes, to drive us closer to the Lord’s lotus feet. We know that nothing can prevent any one of us from becoming a pure devotee of the Lord if we sincerely want to become pure. We take shelter at your lotus feet.

*Your humble servants at ISKCON Miami, Florida, USA.*

## Mississippi

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your divine appearance! All glories to you!

Our beloved spiritual master, founder of our institutional connection to God, please be merciful to us and chastise us so that our faults will heal and we may rise up in our effulgent spiritual bodies.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for accepting us as your disciples. Thank you for creating the International Society for Krishna Consciousness to engage us in saving the world from Kali by offering everyone Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for everything you’ve done to serve our spiritual lives. May we forever be grateful to you, and may we forever have your divine service to contemplate, speak about, and put into action. May we always have the association of one another, and most of all may we learn how to live and serve together without bickering, fighting, and quarreling over every cotton-picking thing there is. May we learn to love and to forgive and to serve without reservation. May we learn to love unconditionally, like you, and may we learn to care for one another as devotees are meant to do.

Please allow us to always have your lotus feet to contemplate and serve. Open up our hearts so that we may see you and Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa sitting together as the Supreme Lord and His most confidential servant. May we always have your *darśana,* and may we always have your dear ISKCON to serve. May we help ISKCON become more and more like you want it to be—the supreme institutional representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead and his beloved devotees.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, a thousand times, again and again. Please whip us to get out of *māyā* and onto the streets, hearing and chanting and crying out the *mahā-mantra*. Please direct us to your next marathon as we humbly attempt to serve your grand expedition of pure *bhakti-yoga*. Thank you, thank you, thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

With love and devotion in service to you in separation,  
*Your servants at ISKCON’s centers in Mississippi, USA.*

## Montreal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

Words fail us when we are called upon to speak of your greatness. Tenaciously determined to execute the orders of your Guru Mahārāja, His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja, you renounced all that is dear to an ordinary human being: family, friends, and the pursuit of wealth, fame, and honor. You even sacrificed in the fire of devotion the shelter of Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma. Turning a deaf ear to the dire warnings about your health, you crossed the oceans, an elderly Vaiṣṇava with an indefatigable desire to save conditioned souls like us, who burn in the forest fire of lust, shamelessly serving the unlimited desires of the mind and senses.

Were you with us only twelve years? How wondrous is Lord Kṛṣṇa’s empowerment of His pure devotee! Like a thunderbolt upon the back of the beast of Kali, your preaching struck a mortal blow against the materialistic Western civilization, and suddenly young people began to chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* read *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and transform themselves into Vaikuṇṭha men and women. Just as Lord Nityānanda took pity on Jagāi and Mādhāi, you cried loudly over the fate of the earth in the hands of the materialistic demons of atheism and impersonalism. Today, by your mercy, millions are chanting the holy name, reading your books, and saving themselves and their families from the clutches of birth and death. Hundreds of thousands of men and women are serving your divine ISKCON mission, the mission of fulfilling Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s prophecy that in the Age of Iron there shall bloom the golden era of the chanting of the holy name, which will create a renewed and re-spiritualized human society.

Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī and the other brilliant moons of the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava *sampradāya* smile with pleasure at your accomplishments, and the three worlds are amazed. How jubilant is Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, who rejoices that Lord Gaurāṅga’s prediction has been fulfilled: *pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma / sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*. \[*CB Antya-khaṇḍa* 4.126\]

To your very last breath, the order of your Guru Mahārāja was your staff of life! On this, the auspicious day of your appearance in this world, may you now empower your followers to take to heart your instructions. The only way to begin to repay our debt to you is to act on the instructions you gave us, without deviation or dilution.

We remain hopeful that by serving you sincerely we will be able by your mercy to take shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your humble servants in the ISKCON Montreal yātrā, Canada.*

## Mumbai (Chowpatty)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

By your causeless mercy we have the good fortune of being a part of this wonderful society that you founded for our benefit. We are indebted to you for all you have done for us and for humanity at large. There is no way we can repay this debt. However, as a token of our gratitude we wish to render some service to try to please you.

Not having had the good fortune of seeing you in person or being able to render any direct service to you in person, we still have the opportunity of serving you by serving your disciples and servants. We are thankful to them and to you for giving us this wonderful opportunity.

Although I am not qualified, this year I had the opportunity to render some service by accepting the responsibility of being the Secretary to the GBC body. In the process of rendering this service, I have had the rare opportunity of working very closely with many of your senior disciples and getting their holy association. I am grateful to them for guiding me and tolerating me.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you always said Bombay is your office. For your pleasure and according to the desire of the GBC body, we have now established a GBC secretariat office in Bombay.

With your blessings and the guidance from my superiors, I shall try to do whatever is necessary to carry out my services in this capacity to the best of my ability.

Once again, thank you for everything you have given us.

*Your servants in Chowpatty, Mumbai, India.*  
(written by Rādhā-Krishna dāsa)

## Mumbai (Juhu)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In the *Bhagavad-gītā* (13.8) Lord Kṛṣṇa says that part of the process of knowledge is *ācāryopāsanam,* to approach a bona fide spiritual master. By your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so many persons are approaching you through your books and for the first time in their lives participating in the process of real knowledge.

Knowledge that leads us to the conclusion that Kṛṣṇa is everything is rarely understood, even after millions of births. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are opening the door to the absolute truth, allowing so many to step out of the darkness and into the light of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Please bless us to be an instrument in your hand so we can assist you in your service to Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura.

Please help us understand that spreading the message of Lord Caitanya is our only business and true happiness.

*Your servants at ISKCON Juhu, Mumbai, India.*

## Nagpur

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* movement, which is spreading everywhere by your mercy!

While in Māyāpur in 1975, shortly after the Gaura Pūrṇimā festival, you corresponded with Mr. Roy, a gentleman from Nagpur. Welcoming his desire to have a temple open here, you enthusiastically wrote back,

The process of opening a center somewhere is that we go there and hold classes for one week on the teachings of Bhagavad-gita, and if the people like, then we’ll open a center. Actually, the teachings of Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu that in every town and village there will be a center of Krishna Consciousness, is essential. People must co-operate. If not, then they will have to suffer. So, I am very glad that you have come forward, being one of the leaders of the cultural community. I am very much enthused by this. Our ideology is standard. It is not anything new, but it is very old. It is not anything concocted. *ajo nityah sasvato ’yam purano na hanyate hanyamane sarire*, The living entity is unborn, eternal, ever-existing, undying and primeval. He is not slain when the body is slain.

Eventually ISKCON Nagpur opened in 1984. The progress has been slow. In your letter you stressed hearing and preaching. This is precisely what we have been endeavoring for this year. And just as you felt enthused by reading Mr. Roy’s call for Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Nagpur, we similarly are feeling enlivened as we begin experiencing positive feedback.

Our emphasis has been twofold: improving the management and enhancing the hearing and chanting of the holy name and *kṛṣṇa-kathā*. While the temple board and finance committee are running the practical aspects of the temple, preachers do house programs and hold weekly *sat-saṅgas* at three different places in town. The youth forum is growing, and college preaching is developing. Book distribution has been revived. The first issue of the ISKCON Nagpur Newsletter is at the printer’s.

For quite some time, the practice of going out on *harināma* had been dropped, but we are now finding new life by calling out for mercy through the city streets every Saturday. Our sense of hearing is being re-invigorated thanks to the additional speakers introduced in the temple classes. We are challenged to increase our study and make better presentations. We opened a library, as well as a tape ministry, through which we are promoting the hearing of your classes.

At the temple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha shine forth, revealing Their ever-increasing and ever-new splendor, daily appearing in elegant home-made outfits and nice decorations. Unexpectedly, and to our great delight, Śrī Girirāja came to us in January. We welcomed Him with *abhiṣeka, kīrtana,* and numerous *bhoga* offerings. Everyone agrees that He brought with Him a special sweetness, auspiciousness, and unity.

We offer all these humble attempts at your lotus feet and beg that you may consider us your servants. Please excuse the lack of expertise we have displayed in the past. We are eager to fulfill your desire for Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Nagpur. By your mercy only can we hope for further progress and success. We take strength from our faith in your words and your presence among us when we preach and follow your instructions.

On this auspicious day we beg for your blessings to become fixed in our *sādhana* and loving toward one another, so we may become qualified to impress your eternal message upon the suffering people of Nagpur and the surrounding areas. Our target for next year is to obtain a piece of land where you may happily reside at the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha and Śrī Girirāja, and irresistibly attract everyone to Their devotional service.

Your whole energy you relentlessly spent in fanning the spark of spirit in people of all walks of life. Again and again we offer our obeisances unto you, by whose desire we are today engaged in establishing Lord Caitanya’s mission in Nagpur. Please continue to fan our spark of life so we may remain always enthused and never deviate from your service.

*Your humble servants at ISKCON Nagpur, India.*

## New Badarik Ashram

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In you we trust,  
Despite the fact  
We’re full of lust.

No selfish motives,  
Only loving care,  
Such a personality  
Is more than rare.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You have come  
And removed our fear,  
Your philosophical teachings  
Have made it so clear.

To chant, dance,  
Feast, and preach,  
Thus the whole world  
Your mercy will reach.

Praying to make  
Your life’s mission our goal  
Is the only thing  
That will satisfy our soul.

“Don’t waste time,”  
we hear you say,  
yet foolishly we do,  
day after day.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
forgive our neglect,  
in your association  
we will become perfect.

Read, study, distribute books  
Far and wide,  
This will only be done  
with you by our side.

The books are like bombs  
That mystically land  
In the most unique places,  
even a garbage can.

And then the stories  
Begin to unfold,  
As pastimes of devotees  
Joining are told.

It’s all in your books;  
Lets put it into practice.  
This is all your gift,  
It is truly matchless.

You asked us to show  
Our love by cooperation,  
And serve within  
Your transcendental organization.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Our debt we cannot repay,  
But we can certainly try  
In every way.

Please, please keep us  
At your lotus feet.  
Only this will make  
Our lowly lives complete.

*Your aspiring servants, the devotees of New Badarik Ashram, Denver, USA.*

## New Biharvan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories, all glories to Your Divine Grace!

I am writing this offering on Lord Varāha’s appearance day. Tomorrow is Lord Nityānanda’s appearance day. It is appropriate to glorify Their Lordships by glorifying you, one of Their pure devotees.

When Hiraṇyākṣa and Hiraṇyakaśipu had plundered and polluted the earth and the oceans so much so that the earth fell into the Garbhodaka Ocean, Lord Varāha had to appear in order to bring her back up from the depths of the ocean. Similarly, Lord Caitanya sent you to lift all of us—the polluted, degraded, fallen souls—from the ocean of sins and faults in this Kali-yuga.

The nations, governments, social scientists had given up on the youth. You, like Lord Varāha, went into the deepest ghettos and slums to pick them up. Who else but you could have gone to the Bowery and picked up the drug addicts and the wretched from the gutters of America and rest of the world?

As Lord Varāha picked up mother earth, He was being attacked by Hiraṇyākṣa. The Lord gently placed the earth in its position and, granting her protection, started to fight Hiraṇyākṣa with His mace. You gently placed us in the house of ISKCON and provided us with protection. While you nourished our hearts and bodies with *harināma* and *prasādam,* you fought with the agents of Kali—the scientists, the Māyāvādīs, and the atheists—with the mace of your purports, just so we could go back home, back to Godhead.

Lord Nityānanda forgave the transgressions of Jagāi and Mādhāi. They gave Him broken bottles of wine. He gave them the holy name. He still forgave them and implored Lord Caitanya to deliver them. We are no different than those two brothers. As Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura says:

*nitāiyer caraṇa satya, tāhāra sevaka nitya*  
*nitāi-pada sadā koro āśa*  
*narottama boro dukhī, nitāi more koro sukhī*  
*rākho rāṅgā-caraṇera pāśa*

**“The lotus feet of Nityānanda are not illusion; they are a fact. One who engages in the transcendental loving service of Nityānanda is also transcendental. Always try to catch the lotus feet of Lord Nityānanda. This Narottama dāsa is very unhappy; therefore I am praying to Lord Nityānanda to make me happy. My dear Lord, please keep me close to Your lotus feet.”**

Since you are a pure representative of Lord Nityānanda, this verse of Śrīla Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura’s applies to you also. For us, your lotus feet are the truth and their service is our goal of life. Please keep us near your lotus feet.

Thank you for giving us a new lease on life, and a chance to go back home, back to Godhead.

*Your fallen servants in New Biharavan, Boise, Idaho, USA.*  
(written by Anantarūpa dāsa)

## New Delhi (Main)

Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have constructed a beautiful temple as an offering to Your Divine Grace. Every day lots of people visit the temple and become Kṛṣṇa conscious. This was your desire—to have a gorgeous temple in South Delhi. The temple has become a very good medium for preaching. To further enhance our preaching programs, we now have an animatronics theater, where a diorama of Kṛṣṇa speaks the *Bhagavad-gītā* to a diorama of Arjuna. We also have a museum, where we display The Mahābhārata Experience, The Rāmāyaṇa Experience, The Caitanya-caritāmṛta Experience, and The Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam Experience. Lots of people visit the animatronics theater and the museum daily.

We are trying to fulfill your cherished desire to distribute lots of *prasādam* both to the needy and the upper class through our Food for Life program, Govinda’s Restaurant, and Sunday Feast program.

During the last book distribution marathon, we distributed over 40,000 big books and mahā big books to please Your Divine Grace. As you said, book distribution is your life and soul.

We are also staging big festivals and Ratha-yātrās at various places throughout the year.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength to further spread the message of Lord Caitanya and his mission. We also seek your blessings so we will all work cooperatively, with love and trust, and in this way continue to serve you to the best of our ability.

*Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Pārthasārathi Mandir, New Delhi, India.*

## New Delhi (Noida)

*ohe! vaiṣṇaba ṭhākura, doyāra sāgara,*  
*e dāse koruṇā kori’*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

It is by your mercy that we are trying to know Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa and Lord Caitanya. It’s only your magnanimous heart that overflowed and spread its kindness upon all of us conditioned souls. There seems absolutely no other way, no other way, no other way besides your lotus feet to attain the perfection of life.

However little advanced we are today in the transcendental field of *bhakti,* and whatever advancement we will make in the future, is all due to your causeless mercy. There is no doubt about it. Just as Śukadeva Gosvāmī says in the *Bhāgavatam*, *nirṇītam*: “it’s already been decided” that constant hearing and chanting of the holy names of the Lord is the doubtless and fearless way of success for all. Similarly, it’s already been decided that Your Divine Grace is the only way back to Godhead.

Your mercy is exhibited in your instructions, which are just the right dose for critically ill patients like us. In fact, your instructions are Vedic truths which allow one who follows or tries to follow them to feel the presence of Guru and Gaurāṅga. May these instructions be forever our most valuable treasure.

You have kindly engaged us in the preaching mission of Lord Caitanya. Please bless us so that we may be able to fulfill your desire: “Preaching is the essence” (both to ourselves and to others). Our lives are an utter waste if we do not preach this divine message all around the world to the best of our ability, both by our deeds and our words. We need your blessings so we may received Kṛṣṇa’s empowerment in this endeavor, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for as it is said, *kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana.*

Kṛṣṇa’s *śakti* flows to the disciple through the channel of the guru, who is you, “the *ācārya* of this age.” How can we even hope to attain that *śakti* without you or your pure representatives? We must all endeavor up to our last breath of this short and unfortunate life to try to surrender to your mercy in the form of your instructions. In this way we may become pure and competent instruments in the hands of Guru and Gaurāṅga.

E dāse koruṇā kori’.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!!

*Your servants in Noida, New Delhi, India.*

## New Dvārakā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**The Captain of the Ship**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

O blessed Captain, west you came  
From far across the sea,  
The *Jaladuta* bore you here  
On a passage granted free.

That voyage, long and tedious,  
Had made you very ill,  
You dreamed that Kṛṣṇa rowed the boat  
And ocean swells were stilled.

As Kṛṣṇa’s representative  
You saw no East nor West,  
To you, we were all suffering,  
Your presence made us blessed.

In frail old age you ventured forth  
With guru as your guide,  
Just forty rupees and your books  
And Kṛṣṇa by your side.

You’re now the captain of a ship  
Bound for Goloka Dhām,  
With stories of the pure love there  
You urge us homeward bound.

With you to steer, we feel secure  
Through ever-surging waves,  
The life-boats of your books we cast  
And drowning souls are saved.

The winds of Vedic knowledge blow,  
This human life’s a chance  
To cross the sea of birth and death  
And join the Lord’s own dance.

Your books, the basis of our lives,  
Are beacons shining bright,  
As mariners of old were guided  
By the stars of night.

Your preaching is the essence clear,  
The truth of God and soul,  
Utility the principle,  
And service is the goal.

Your mercy’s there for everyone  
Who follows on this course,  
Through tide and current on we go,  
Your purity’s the force.

Though tossed around by winter squalls,  
We hold fast to your feet,  
But neither storm nor preying sharks  
Can sink your sturdy fleet.

Though phantom ships may pass us by  
And try to lure us on,  
They ply a very crooked course  
And sing a siren’s song.

Your legacy’s a treasure chest  
With vigilance we guard,  
To handle and deliver it  
Unaltered and unmarred.

On board your ship we sing and dance  
And serve the Lord’s sweet form.  
Though lost and fallen once were we,  
Our lives are now transformed.

So many sincere souls are here,  
They serve with love and faith,  
And just as you risked all to preach  
They follow in your wake.

Though some may come to rock the boat,  
We can’t let them dissuade;  
We know that Kṛṣṇa’s rowing still  
This boat that you have made.

Please let us stay on board your ship,  
And guide us evermore.  
No more a drifter cast at sea,  
We’re bound for yonder shore.

O blessed Captain, west you came,  
Your mission deep and grave,  
To pioneer Caitanya’s path  
The whole world thus to save.

*Your ever-aspiring servants in New Dvārakā Dhāma, Los Angeles, California, USA.*  
(written by Guṇāvatī-devī dāsī)

## New Gokula-dhām

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet. We roll in that dust repeatedly and beg to never be separated from it. All glories to this most auspicious appearance-day celebration—Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in attempting to write this offering, we realize that we are lacking in our ability to properly compose a paper appropriate for such an exalted and transcendental occasion. We are bereft of a vocabulary of eloquent words, and our grammatical strength is weak at best. Furthermore, when the fingers are poised for typing, the mind is often blank, and when the heart is inspired to write, the computer is usually off!

We wonder how it will be possible to properly glorify a Vaikuṇṭha personality who is beyond our limited spiritual experience and comprehension. We pray to the previous *ācāryas* that we may be endowed with at least a few appropriate words to offer today.

To move beyond the limitations of a materially contaminated consciousness and reach the state of unadulterated transcendental consciousness, one needs the merciful help of the bona fide spiritual master, whose mercy is bestowed upon the faithful disciple who properly worships and serves his guru.

By your self-realized instructions, causeless mercy, and transcendental example we can achieve the highest stage of self-realization, which cannot be obtained through mental speculation or self-ascension. A devotee achieves the perfectional stage only by the mercy of the guru who reveals himself externally. Furthermore, it is by the guru’s merciful revelations, both externally and internally, that a *sādhaka* (devotee) may make advancement on the path of *sādhana-bhakti,* gradually culminating in the highest perfectional stage—*kṛṣṇa-prema*.

As mentioned above, the guru manifests in a twofold form—internally and externally—and those *bhaktas* who have realized this *tattva,* or truth, sincerely pray,

*naivopayanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam ṛddha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*yo ’ntar bahis tanu-bhṛtām aśubhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caittya-vapuṣā sva-gatiṁ vyanakti*

**“O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features—externally as the** ***ācārya*** **and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.29.6)**

The bona fide guru cleanses the dust from the heart of the sincere disciple by showing him the way of devotional service, which begins with hearing and chanting about the all-auspicious Supreme Personality of Godhead. As the disciple’s heart becomes cleansed of contamination, realization of his true nature begins to unfold, and the darkness of his ignorance is dissipated.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have boldly declared, “Godhead is light, nescience is darkness. Where there is Godhead, there is no nescience!” To see that abode of Godhead (Vaikuṇṭha) one needs first to purify his senses by means of the mercy of the *bhāgavata*. That mercy arrives in two features: the person *bhāgavata* and the book *bhāgavata, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam—*“the torchlight of knowledge.”

Originally, that torchlight was manifest in the form of answers to the four questions asked by Lord Brahmā. Lord Brahmā in turn revealed those answers to Nārada Muni, who in turn revealed them to his disciple Śrīla Vyāsadeva. Śrīla Vyāsadeva delivered them to his son Śukadeva Gosvāmī, and Śukadeva Goswami revealed that science of Godhead to the dying Mahārāja Parīkṣit. Śrīla Sūta Gosvāmī made further elaborations, and it became known as the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*.

Now, thousands of years later, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have revealed that same supreme science of Godhead to us, with further elaborations (in the form of your purports). This is the system of receiving transcendental knowledge—*ācāryavān puruṣo veda,* or knowing things as they are through the guidance of the disciplic succession of Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas*.

By receiving transcendental knowledge in this way, the sincere disciple will eventually be relieved from the embarrassment of being covered by the three material modes and be re-established in his original constitutional nature of *śuddha-sattva,* or pure goodness. A disciple who advances by the mercy of a *bhāgavata* humbly prays,

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

When the disciple is sufficiently advanced, his natural tendency is to express his indebtedness to his spiritual master, as well as to hear and chant about the guru’s many transcendental attributes, especially in the association of other devotees.

Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā offers an opportunity to relish the sublime and exalted nature of your personality through the experiences and realizations of your many followers. By hearing such transcendental descriptions, your followers become more and more inspired, and from such inspiration their determination to pursue the path of *bhakti* increases. We relish such pastimes and wish to hear them again and again.

Though you are praised in Vedic literature as the external manifestation of the *caitya-guru* (the Lord in the heart), our minds are more attracted by your personal exchanges with us as a caring father, friend, and ever well-wisher. It is for this reason that we would like to share one of your pastimes, which took place in the early ’70s in Los Angles, when the devotees in the art department had created a diorama of Kṛṣṇa and Arjuna on the chariot.

You were visiting the temple at the time, and the devotees were eager to serve you by recreating this wonderful three-dimensional scene from the *Bhagavad-gītā*. However, they weren’t quite ready for you yet, when one of the devotees glanced out the window and noticed that you were on the way—ready or not!

Then all of a sudden, as if knowing their anxiety, you paused in the middle of the street, turned, and walked in another direction. “Kṛṣṇa’s arrangement,” they happily declared!

Fervently they picked up the pace and rushed to complete it immediately. Again, a few minutes later, you appeared in view and were quickly moving toward them. As you reached the threshold of the studio, the last necklace fell into place on the Lord’s body. The door swung open. You walked in. An extension cord draped across the entrance was lifted, and you stepped forward without a break in stride. The events unfolded just like a play produced by an expert director. Such an amazing scene!

After making your way across the floor filled with disciples paying obeisances, you were comfortably seated in a rocking chair in the corner, which had been placed there for your comfort. As you sat looking at the diorama (which was located approximately ten feet in front of you, and centered in the room), you began to gently rock back and forth, *japa* beads in hand, chanting softly, “Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa .” Your disciples stood speechless, captivated by your every gesture, every murmur. “. Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare.” Such nectar! Such suspense! What were your thoughts? What were you going to say? Your disciples, suspended in complete rapture, hung on to every moment.

Then your rocking paused and you spoke with a look of apparent displeasure. “Kṛṣṇa looks a little old.” The hearts of your servants sank—they had failed to please their spiritual master. There wasn’t a sound in the room. No smiling faces. No “Haribols.” Nothing.

After a moment, you continued to speak. They listened, and you explained that although Kṛṣṇa was a great-grandfather at the time, He appeared in a youthful form of about twenty to twenty-five years of age. Your words were like a massage—gentle and rejuvenating. You weren’t displeased or disappointed! You were lovingly correcting and guiding them to the Supreme Truth.

After some time, you continued your gentle rhythmic rocking while fingering your *japa* beads within their bag and quietly chanting “Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.”

A moment later you paused again, slightly smiled, and with a pleasant tone remarked that Arjuna looked very attractive! You said that his attraction was due to his deep devotion to Kṛṣṇa, the reservoir and bestower of all attractive qualities. Your words rejuvenated the atmosphere and enlivened everyone present.

Suddenly the room resounded with, “Haribol! Haribol!” The artists’ hearts were uplifted and their hopes restored by your encouragement and soothing words. The fulfillment of their desire to somehow please you had been achieved. Success was theirs!

Remembering the different ways you moved among us, and your compassionate and loving nature, makes us forget your supreme position as the external manifestation of God in the heart. Our minds return instead to your various pastimes and your exchanges with us. Our thoughts are absorbed in remembering your morning walks, your expressive gestures, and even the captivating way you played the *karatālas*, holding them as if they were dancers on the stage of your hands!

There is no difference between your *vapu* (physical presence) and your *vāṇī* (instructions). Both are on the eternal spiritual platform. If we sincerely wish to associate with you, we simply have to follow your instructions. The only requirement for eternal association is adherence to those instructions. Sixteen rounds. The four regs. Read your books. Etc. If we do that, we are associating with you personally. You are nondifferent from your instructions, and if we follow those instructions then we will live eternally with you. However, if we distance ourselves from them, we distance ourselves from you.

In this connection, we would like to quote a letter you wrote to Cidānanda Prabhu on November 25, 1973:

“The spiritual Master lives forever by his divine instruction and the disciple lives with him.” Because I have always served my Guru Maharaja and followed his teachings I am now even never separated from him. Sometimes Maya may come and try to interfere, but we must not falter, we must always follow the chalked out path laid down by the great acharyas, and in the end you will see.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for being the perfect example of how a disciple should serve and follow his spiritual master. Though our attempts are often flawed, we pray that you may continue to accept them, and keep us forever with you.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your followers at New Gokula Dhāma, Vancouver, BC, Canada.*  
(written by Bhāva dāsa)

## New Goloka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on your divine appearance day!

Who can imagine a world without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda? For centuries the treasure of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement lay hidden from the world. During these centuries “Western Civilization” made huge advancements in materialistic science and technology. The West spread its influence around the world, attempting to convince all people that true happiness lay in material advancement. While they were promising unlimited happiness and freedom, they were actually delivering brutal wars, exploitation, and godlessness. But you came into this kingdom of the godless, Śrīla Prabhupāda, outwardly an older Indian man with no money and no influence with big people. You came in 1965 to the mecca of materialistic sin, New York City, with the greatest treasure, the message of Gaura-Nitāi’s divine *saṅkīrtana* movement.

I wish that You may deliver them. Therefore if You so desire their deliverance, then only will they be able to understand Your message.

Somehow or other, O Lord, You have brought me here to speak about You. Now, my Lord, it is up to you to make me a success or failure, as You like.

Only by Your causeless mercy will my words become pure. I am sure that when this transcendental message penetrates their hearts, they will feel gladdened and thus become liberated from all unhappy conditions of life.

How the world has changed since you came to New York in 1965! Yes, there is more degradation, but in the darkness there is the single light of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Millions of people have understood Kṛṣṇa’s message by your divine grace. Kṛṣṇa has made you the *jagad-guru* who has glorified Him with volumes of books translated into scores of languages and distributed in hundreds of countries. Indeed, your pure preaching has engladdened all of our hearts, freeing us from so many miserable engagements in this material world. By your divine grace we have been able to taste the nectar of the holy name.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, nearly forty years since you first set foot in America, we are preaching here in North Carolina by your mercy alone. We are enthused by your words and instructions found in your books. We are nourished by spiritual foodstuffs that you taught us how to prepare. And we are blessed with the association of our Godbrothers and Godsisters, all of whom are the mercy of your International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! On this glorious anniversary of your divine appearance in this world, we, your disciples and grand-disciples at New Goloka, pray to you for the inspiration and strength to expand your preaching mission to all those unfortunate souls who do not yet know the glories of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda! We pray to you for the courage to take the message of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi into the streets with a glorious *saṅkīrtana* party. And we pray never to forget your causeless mercy upon us by allowing us to assist you in your mission to save the fallen conditioned souls, who have forgotten their eternal relationship with the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

*Your servants at New Goloka Dhāma, Hillsborough, NC, USA.*

## New Govardhana Hill (Gurabo)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble offering at your lotus feet. As well-intentioned as this attempt at writing an homage for you may be, I am aware that it is fraught with my numerous failings. As this is the first attempt at such a personal / public offering, I hope to not offend you or anyone else.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have taken Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission to heart and embraced it with the instructions of your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, to go to the West and spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness philosophy in English-speaking, religiously-repressed countries. In that embrace you have exemplified the determination to focus at every moment on the current most important item (or project) wherever you happened to be, while keeping all other projects (worldwide) in a prioritized order within your mind. As opportunities to solve problems manifested by Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s grace, you would assign some qualified disciple to take responsibility to solve it, or you would suddenly apply the solution with amazing grace.

Although this was one of your most significant examples for your G.B.C. body of disciples, who were your general management representatives, it was only the icing on the cake of your glorious devotional activities.

For all your disciples you further exemplified the transcendental art of maintaining your daily routine of *sādhana,* translation, travel, preaching engagements, and project oversight or guidance of every description, simultaneously maintaining a practically continuous open *darśana* for local and visiting disciples, dignitaries, and general guests. Additionally, you always took time to personally answer your disciples’ mail from all over the world.

The list of your accomplishments is well known to all your followers, so much so that some even insist that your personal influence will continue to resonate so strongly over the next 10,000 years (through your books), that *no other guru is needed*—they are that overwhelmed by the strength of your presence in their lives! However, you might say, “That is Kṛṣṇa’s presence, coming in *paramparā*. Plug into *His* energy and *you become guru*!” And they will! After they have worked through their sorrow of separation and found that only responsible action driven by that transcendental energy and guided by *sādhus* and your unadulterated śāstric translations remain to help us transcend these afflictions. All of which simply reveals another way in which you have protected us.

So the list of your benedictions is always increasing. How can we, who live a mere hundred years maximum, enumerate all the solutions your transcendental instructions will provide for the world’s problems over the next 10,000 years? Only history can accurately document this. We can simply try to apply the teachings in your books to solve the problems that tax society today.

Your Bhaktivedanta Institute is actively pursuing interfaith dialog by pointing out the similarities among the world’s religions. It is also pursuing the synthesis of science and religion. Similarly, the BI devotees are also uncovering cover-ups of various scientific discoveries that support the Vedic conclusion. The books they produce are in great demand, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you have opened the people’s eyes.

On so many fronts we are witnessing great strides in the public arena, laying a solid foundation for current and future development of your “house in which the whole world can live.” Preaching centers, temples, and Nāma Haṭṭa and Bhakti Vṛkṣa preaching programs are opening all over the world, providing your books in every language, reaching out to people in every nook and cranny of every town and village.

You have inspired so many older and seasoned disciples to obtain college degrees at some of the most prestigious universities in the world. The purpose is to establish the Vedic College you so frequently spoke of. And for those of us who want to “make the best use of a bad bargain” by using our latent leadership abilities in Kṛṣṇa’s service, you have also inspired additional effort to develop leadership skills so we may take responsible positions in your Society.

In this way you continually harness our abilities in the service of your mission to reveal Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s presence in the daily lives of the people of the modern so-called civilization. Personally, I feel distraught that I have yet to discover my real role in your mission; I know that when I do so I will be more effective in your service. It is in that mood that I hereby submit my humble past efforts at your feet, while praying for your blessings and the blessings of all your sincere followers all over the world. May I continue to seek the essence of their association at your feet, and someday qualify myself to share that association with all others.

*The devotees at New Govardhana Hill, Gurabo, Puerto Rico.*  
(written by Laṅgagaṇeśa dāsa)

## New Govardhana (San Diego)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and unworthy obeisances on this day, the anniversary of your appearance in this world, which marked the true beginning of all our lives and hopes to be freed from the blazing fire of material suffering.

It is very difficult to write an offering on behalf of an entire community, everyone having different realizations and being in different phases of their spiritual lives. I can only try to write this offering based on the lives and service of a few of your grand-disciples, who by default are managing your temple here in San Diego and trying our best to serve your mission.

One day the pages of this book will be filled entirely by your grand-disciples. One day soon it will be we that must carry your mission on our heads and in our hearts. One day we will not have the inconceivable rare mercy and guidance of your dearmost direct disciples. We are so fortunate to be alive and serving in your ISKCON movement during this time, yet we find so many excuses to not surrender or take shelter of your dear disciples. We can’t even imagine or measure the value of their association. To think that one day they will not be there for future generations to take shelter of and hear from firsthand about your glorious pastimes. We must contemplate the seriousness of that, just as one must contemplate the seriousness of one’s death.

In your books you always stress the urgency of developing Kṛṣṇa consciousness and performing devotional service. Somehow we see ourselves forgetting that instruction, taking spiritual life to be a social culture more than a means of deliverance and purification.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bless us over and over again that we can learn to practice and preach the essence of your teachings—pure devotional service as Lord Caitanya revealed it to Rāmānanda Rāya (*Teachings of Lord Caitanya,* Chapters 27 and 28). Please help us value your direct disciples and treasure all the nectar you spoke to them as one values his own material desires. I say that because it is only our material desires and conceptions, which we cling to so tightly, that keep us from hearing and extracting the nectar and applying it to our lives.

Your hand is extending, offering all relief from this world of suffering, yet somehow we don’t grab hold of it. We don’t read, chant, or do as much service as we should. We find so many excuses to not grab hold of your hand. We blame one another or convince ourselves we’re doing enough. The test is how much we are developing our urgency to serve you and deliver others to your lotus feet.

With true sincerity to serve you and the Supreme Lord comes purity of the heart. Naturally one precedes the other. If we can somehow develop true sincerity to spread your movement and teachings, then along with that will come purity. With a pure heart, we can serve your mission unflinchingly and with great urgency.

It is by your mercy alone that we have come together as a family, and it is by your mercy that we will go on as a spiritual family. Please help us to value one another and serve your mission with all our abilities, for there isn’t much time in this world for any of us.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us.

*Your devotees in New Govardhana, San Diego, California, USA.*  
(written by Gāndharvikā-devī dāsī)

## New Guṇḍicā Mandira

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. You are the shelter of the conditioned souls suffering in this world of illusion and pain. Because of your unlimited dedication to your spiritual master, you left the holiest of places on this planet to deliver Kṛṣṇa to the fallen conditioned souls. You had full faith in Kṛṣṇa’s protection because you have full realization of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. You have become Kṛṣṇa’s manifestation of mercy in this world.

You have given us a method to gain an understanding of the nature of this world and a method to regain our eternal relationship with our supreme benefactor, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You have given us Śrī Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, the chanting of the holy names of God. You once said that *kīrtana* is the only safe place in this world. Such is the power of the holy name; such is the clarity of your gift to us.

*harer nāma harer nāma harer nāmaiva kevalam*  
*kalau nāsty eva nāsty eva nāsty eva gatir anyathā*

Any attempt of ours to explain our appreciation to you will be inadequate. We had no qualifications to serve your mission or even to understand your gift. Any understanding we may have gained is also simply by your mercy. By your example in life you constantly demonstrated the qualities of a Vaiṣṇava with simple brilliance and elegance. By your example we have a road map home, back to Godhead.

By your example and because of your divine inspiration the influence of Kali is being reduced and the fallen conditioned souls have a source of hope. You translated *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Bhagavad-gītā,* and *Caitanya-caritāmṛta.* You wrote other books to help us understand the most basic truths of existence. Without your books this world would be an unbearably dark place. Without your books we would have no hope of salvation from illusion. Thank you.

By the divine example that is your life we are inspired to attempt to please you and serve your mission. By your continued mercy, countless persons are able to hear the holy names and thus begin their journey home. What marvelous gifts you have given us! Book distribution, *ārati,* Vaiṣṇava association, *prasādam, saṅkīrtana, darśana* of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa By your mercy we are able to share Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s message with the gentle and ruffians alike—the so-called educated and the admittedly ignorant.

We owe everything to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your attachment to serving the order of your spiritual master has changed the world. The continued distribution of your books continues to change the world. We owe everything to you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to thank you for the oasis that is New Guṇḍicā Mandira, in Boston. By your sincere desire and inspiration, your sincere disciples began to establish a suitable place for Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Their Lordships Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā, and the Divine Couple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīvallabha, to appear in Boston. By your sincere desire and inspiration your disciples and grand-disciples continue to serve Their Lordships with attention to your detailed instructions. Your kindness and mercy manifest in the tireless dedication of these servants. They inspire all who meet them to serve your mission in the Boston area.

So on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance, please continue to give us your mercy so that we may renew our dedication to cultivating the attitude of a humble servant. Only by your mercy can we possibly achieve any attraction for devotional life.

You live forever in your divine instructions, and your followers live with you in that most special of places, in the presence of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīvallabha’s New Guṇḍicā Mandira, Boston, Massachusetts, USA.*

## New Jagannātha Purī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

In this Age of Kali, those who take shelter of your lotus feet are the most fortunate. *Brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva / guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja.* (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 19.151) Our lives become meaningful when we come in touch with Lord Kṛṣṇa by serving your lotus feet. Otherwise we are lost in the intricacies of this material world.

This San Francisco Bay area is bereft of all good qualities. By your mercy Lord Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Lady Subhadrā have appeared in this fallen part of the world and are unfailingly giving benedictions to the conditioned souls. They are most merciful.

You declared here, “I will never die. I shall live in my books, and you will utilize.” In your purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.12.24, Your Divine Grace has clarified the nature of spiritual association:

To follow the instructions of the Lord is to associate with the Lord. *The Lord is not a material object whose presence one has to feel for such association.* It is quite possible to have His association simply by following His instructions because the Lord and His instruction and the Lord and His name, fame, attributes and paraphernalia are all identical with Him, being absolute knowledge. \[italics in original\]

Certainly this applies to Your Divine Grace as well: *you are not a material object*.

You also desired that this temple should become affiliated with the University of California at Berkeley and become an educational center.

Your glories are unlimited and inexhaustible. Every Monday night devotees get a chance to associate with Your Divine Grace in the form of your video lectures. In one of those lectures, you gave the example of a tape recording. The tape is different from the person who spoke it, but when you hear the tape being played again, you begin to hear that person. Then you went on to say, “Similarly, if you record my lecture, the tape is different from me, but when you play the tape and hear the instructions, you will know, ‘Yes, Prabhupāda is here, A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami is here.’” How fortunate for this world!

*Your servants at New Jagannātha Purī, the ISKCON temple in Berkeley, California, USA.*

## New Māyāpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā ceremony!

From your New Māyāpur, in the middle of France, we are writing to you to express our deep appreciation for your mercy of engaging us in your wonderful mission of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Considering our lack of qualification, it appears evident that the project is able to go on only by your “unceasing flow of mercy,” as you wrote for your own spiritual master in 1936.

In the last lecture you gave here, in August 1976, you said in conclusion, “If you protect the cows and the brahminical culture, you will be happy and your country will be happy.” Since that time, by the inconceivable arrangements of Lord Kṛṣṇa, more and more people in this country are turning toward a more sattvic life: the number of butchers has decreased by two-thirds in thirty years, and it is now forbidden to smoke in public rooms.

There are other good signs of people waking up little by little. As you said, “Every improvement in Kali-yuga is coming from the spreading of the *saṅkīrtana* movement.” And this *saṅkīrtana* movement is spreading by your pure devotion only, although you wrote, “I have no devotion and no knowledge, but I have firm faith in my Guru Mahārāja’s instructions.” So dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us have a little drop of your pure faith in Guru and Kṛṣṇa; then success is there. In this way please let us improve all together, in a nice cooperative spirit, according to your instructions and will. Then, for your pleasure and the pleasure of all your followers, we will be able to inspire all the citizens of this country to know, appreciate, and practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

This holy *dhāma* of New Māyāpur is a manifestation of your service to our Supreme Best Friend, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, so we want to improve and revive services like Deity Worship, *harināma*, book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, the bhakta program, *gurukula,* Vaiṣṇava seminars, mother cow protection, congregational preaching, administration, reception, self-sufficiency, good living facilities, and life-long maintenance for devotees. For your transcendental pleasure, please allow us to bring all these services to their best levels.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly forgive our offenses and shortcomings, and please kindly accept our little service. In this way we will have more opportunities to advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. This world is a strong prison house, so how can we really be happy without pleasing you?

All glories to Your Divine Grace! All success to your divine mission!

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your servants at New Māyāpur dhāma, France.*  
(written by Madhvācārya dāsa)

## New Nandagram

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your exalted status among all Vaiṣṇavas is not rivaled now, nor shall it be in the future. This must be clearly understood by any serious follower of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. Even the slightest attempt to minimize your achievements in the service of the *saṅkīrtana* movement will constitute a great Vaiṣṇava *aparādha*.

The desire of Lord Caitanya was to spread the chanting of the holy names of the Lord to every town and village of the world. The Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana, headed by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, were directly instructed by Lord Caitanya in the science of chanting the holy names of the Lord. They gave up opulent and lofty positions to dedicate themselves to the life-long task of writing volumes of books on this science, books meant to be distributed all over the world. But they left that task of world book distribution for their devout followers. Śrīla Prabhupāda, though your contemporaries hesitated to take advantage of this opportunity to please the Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana, you alone were fully awake and fully dedicated to the mission of distributing these *ācāryas’* books to all mankind. By successfully accomplishing this task, you have no doubt revealed your position as the dearmost follower of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī and his associates. Considering that Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī and his associates are also counted among the confidential associates of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, your glories cannot be fully comprehended, since you are the object of their full affection due to fulfilling their innermost desires.

The *ācāryas* of the four *sampradāyas—*Viṣṇu Svāmī, Nimbārkācārya, Rāmānuja Swami, and Madhvācārya—and all their followers must also be constantly praising your glories. All their teachings were united, complemented, and brought to the supreme stage of perfection through Lord Caitanya’s philosophy of *acintya-bhedābheda-tattva,* which you have made easily available and easy to comprehend by all peoples of the world. You often said that your only credit was that you did not change the teachings of the Lord. But your humility cannot hide the truth that your glories far exceed this vital input. You not only took the supreme transcendental knowledge—unchanged—to all the peoples of the world, but you made everyone love you and thus embrace the ancient teachings. Who else in the history of the world has ever melted so many stonelike hearts, in so many countries of the world, while giving the most valuable gift of love of God in charity? No one can equal such inconceivable compassion and expertise. By the grace of the Lord, His dearmost devotee can accomplish even more than the Lord can. Śrīla Prabhupāda, the Lord no doubt wants your name to be also glorified in every town and village of the world.

Many *ācāryas* have worked very hard to present the flawless teachings and culture of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu in all its intricate details. They have also worked very hard to keep those teachings from being polluted by deviant sects, and to attract the conditioned souls to take up the spirit of those teachings by reviving their loving relationships with the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Jīva Gosvāmī took up the task of systematically spreading the teachings of the other Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana, and their followers such as Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja, throughout India. He did this with the help of Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura, Śrīnivāsa Ācārya, and Śyāmānanda Prabhu. In the course of time the Vaiṣṇava culture became almost lost, but it was again kick-started by Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura. Śrīla Baladeva Vidyābhūṣaṇa defended the authenticity of the Gauḍīya *sampradāya* by compiling the *Govinda-bhāṣya* commentary on the *Vedānta-sūtra*. Śrīla Jagannātha dāsa Bābājī and Śrīla Gaura Kiśora dāsa Bābājī kept the pure teachings intact within their hearts and through their instructions to their disciples. The Gauḍīya *sampradāya’s* complete teachings and culture were again kick-started by the tireless efforts of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura. He also re-established the full reverence the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava tradition deserves as the topmost transcendental engagement. With great force he pioneered the spreading of Vaiṣṇava culture beyond the shores of India. Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī dedicated his entire energy to completing the task his father had begun. He defeated the false philosophies that had sprung up throughout India, which had previously threatened to pollute or hide the Vaiṣṇava truths. Then he made all arrangements to send the pure Vaiṣṇava teachings and culture to the rest of the world.

Finally, after the thousands of years of preparation by the Lord and his greatest devotees, the stage was set for worldwide distribution and propagation of the pure science of love of God in its highest form. But which great Vaiṣṇava would be entrusted with this most sacred mission? Which expert devotee could guarantee success in this mission and thus bring joy to the hearts of Lord Caitanya and all the *ācāryas* who had dedicated their all-in-all for this goal? Which great Vaiṣṇava would go far beyond the call of duty to complete the task at hand, no matter what personal loss or inconvenience was endured? Which great Vaiṣṇava possessed all the good qualities of the demigods to the highest degree, which could attract people from all over the world to take up the Vaiṣṇava teachings? Which great Vaiṣṇava was the personification of the Vaiṣṇava philosophy? Which great Vaiṣṇava had unflinching faith in the order of his guru, all the previous *ācāryas*, and the Supreme Lord and would thus be blessed with the shelter of the spiritual potency in order to successfully execute this unmatched transcendental service? Which great Vaiṣṇava would fulfill the prophecy of the Lord, that in every town and village His holy name would be chanted? That greatest of Vaiṣṇavas, who gives transcendental pleasure to the Lord and all His pure devotees, is His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

To further underscore the rarity of your monumental achievement, one need also consider the rarity of the appearance of Lord Caitanya and the rarity of the Lord’s gift of *kṛṣṇa-prema*. Only once in a day of Brahmā does Lord Caitanya come to distribute *kṛṣṇa-prema* to the undeserving conditioned souls. A greater gift is never so liberally offered by any other incarnation of God. Considering that you were empowered by the Lord to distribute love of God further afield than even the Lord traveled, and to more degraded and fallen souls than the Lord encountered, there can be no doubt that your service to the Lord shall be unique and unrivaled for trillions of years. All devotees who take advantage of Lord Caitanya’s movement during this golden era of 10,000 years should offer their homage and respects to you and learn from you the method and mood of service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, there is no doubt that you are embraced within the hearts of the Lord and all the pure devotees of the Lord, for you are the most confidential servitor of the Lord. Yet you are also embraced within the hearts of the fallen conditioned souls rotting within this world birth after birth. For you came and saved us when no one else did.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*The devotees of the New Nandagram farm community in Bambra, Victoria, Australia.*  
(written by Gaṅgeśvara dāsa)

## New Nīlācala

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble *daṇḍavats* under the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you!

In your purport to text three of the *Upadeśāmṛta* you write, “One should accept this opportunity to return home, back to Godhead, very enthusiastically. Without enthusiasm, one cannot be successful.” And once, when commenting on the word enthusiasm (*utsāha*) used in the *Upadeśāmṛta,* you gave yourself as an example: “How could I have come to America at such an old age unless I was enthusiastic?” You were enthusiastic to come to America because your guru ordered you to, and when for a whole year you got almost no response in the USA, you remained patient (*dhairyāt*) but still enthusiastic. That enthusiasm can be heard on tape recordings of your lectures on the Lower East Side, where your voice often cracks with earnestness as you deliver the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the power to be always enthusiastic like you, and in that way we will be able to spread the message of Kṛṣṇa throughout our country. We know that genuine disciples become enthusiastic in devotional service, but we are very fallen and we are not genuine, so please be merciful to us, because we are in devotional service only by your causeless mercy.

Begging to become your sincere devotees,  
*Your servants at New Nīlācala Mandir, Guadalajara, Mexico.*  
(written by Premānanda dāsa)

## New Orleans

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this very auspicious day of your divine appearance, we wish to offer our prostrated humble obeisance at the dust of your lotus feet. May their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhākānta, Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā, and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi keep blessing us so that we can continue serving your mission in New Orleans, which is known as Saṅkīrtana Purī Dhāma.

We all have come to take shelter at this great mission so that we can be saved from the hands of Māyā by serving the divine lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. You have given us Lord Jagannātha, Śrī Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā, who are unlimitedly merciful. By allowing us to distribute *mahā-prasādam,* Lord Jagannātha is giving His unlimited mercy to the people of this part of the world. You have also given us Their most merciful Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhākānta, who are kindly staying here and allowing us to worship Them.

Most of us living here in Saṅkīrtana Purī Dhāma are your grand-disciples, disciples of your dear disciple Jayapatāka Swami. We have come from different parts of the world for so many reasons. But how wonderful it is that Lord Kṛṣṇa has kept us engaged here to work together in co-operation and give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others.

We are trying to improve our preaching work by organizing Bhakti Vriksha programs regularly. Some new young boys and girls are accepting Kṛṣṇa consciousness sincerely. Besides all the hard struggle that we have had to go through, we still have been able to maintain and improve our Deity worship. There are more guests then ever at the regular Sunday Feast program. We have been assigned by our GBC, Bīr Krishna dāsa Goswami, to renovate your temple. This will definitely be quite a task. So far we have put in a new roof, general repair work is going on, and new painting will start soon. After all this work is accomplished, we want to install a *sudarṣana cakra* on the top of your temple. We also have a desire to restart our traditional Ratha-yātrā, with the help of the Festival of India. I hope this will increase our preaching and satisfy Your Divine Grace.

Please keep blessing us eternally so that we can be an instrument of your mission.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your eternally grateful grandsons and granddaughters in New Orleans, Louisiana, USA.*

## New Pānihāṭi

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to you!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are so grateful for the opportunity you have provided for us here in Atlanta, and we pray for the determination to fulfill your vision of creating a center to train a class of *brāhmaṇas* who can educate the world in the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We are also very happy that you have sent your dear disciple Hridayānanda dāsa Goswami to guide and assist us here in developing an ISKCON educational center similar to a religious seminary. We plan to work very closely together so that our combined efforts will produce a truly transcendental result. You wanted your followers to cooperate in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and we hope to demonstrate this cooperative spirit over the next few years.

Our goal is to fully restore your property to the standards of the Druid Hills Historic District, with emphasis on attracting the local community. We plan to interface with local professors to create a unique cultural experience for students and other interested visitors.

Many of your disciples and followers who are now seriously taking care of their family responsibilities are again getting excited about preaching and helping make New Pānihāṭi Dhāma the most important preaching and educational center in North America.

It is so amazing how the light of your presence shines so brightly in the hearts of those who do not waver from their commitment to your instructions. Every day we are seeing more and more how people are becoming attracted to you just as we did while you were physically present. As we explain to people about your incredible example of devotion and dedication to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, we see their faith in devotional service grow.

Our hope is to see an army of empowered preachers moving within society and effecting massive changes for the betterment of all living entities. But for this to happen, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we need your divine mercy, since your mercy is all that we are made of. Without your blessings we cannot hope to achieve any measurable success. We are determined to make you proud of us here at New Pānihāṭi Dhāma, and we are prepared to dedicate all our intelligence and resources as a community to this end.

Please continue to watch over us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and bless us with clear realizations of your teachings so that we may give them to others and in this way truly keep you in the center of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

*Your humble servants at New Pānihāṭi Dhāma, Atlanta, Georgia, USA.*

## New Ramaṇ Reti

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Enlightenment—Association—Responsibility**

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*
