You told us that your books would be the law books of the planet for the next 10,000 years, the period of time calculated to be inundated by Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* mercy.

Your books cover all aspects of life and how to proceed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness in any of the *varṇas* or *āśramas*. At ISCOWP we scour your books, tapes, and conversation books for your instructions on cow protection so that we may be aware of your instructions on this most important topic. If I may quote here one of these drops of nectar from *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (8.24.5):

\[Translation\] Śrī Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: O King, for the sake of protecting the cows, *brāhmaṇas,* demigods, devotees, the Vedic literature, religious principles, and principles to fulfill the purpose of life, the Supreme Personality of Godhead accepts the forms of incarnations.

\[Purport\] The Supreme Personality of Godhead generally appears in various types of incarnations to give protection to the cows and *brāhmaṇas*. The Lord is described as *go-brāhmaṇa-hitāya ca;* in other words, He is always eager to benefit the cows and *brāhmaṇas*. When Lord Kṛṣṇa appeared, He purposefully became a cowherd boy and showed personally how to give protection to the cows and calves. Similarly, He showed respect to Sudāmā Vipra, a real *brāhmaṇa*. From the Lord’s personal activities, human society should learn how to give protection specifically to the *brāhmaṇas* and cows. Then the protection of religious principles, fulfillment of the aim of life, and protection of Vedic knowledge can be achieved. Without protection of cows, brahminical culture cannot be maintained; and without brahminical culture, the aim of life cannot be fulfilled. The Lord, therefore, is described as *go-brāhmaṇa-hitāya* because His incarnation is only for the protection of the cows and *brāhmaṇas*. Unfortunately, because in Kali-yuga there is no protection of the cows and brahminical culture, everything is in a precarious position. If human society wants to be exalted, the leaders of society must follow the instructions of *Bhagavad-gītā* and give protection to the cows, the *brāhmaṇas,* and brahminical culture.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have very clearly spelled out from A through Z the importance of the cows and the necessity of their protection. We at ISCOWP pray that we may realize your instructions and put them into practical application on your behalf.

We are eternally grateful for your divine instructions and humbly beg for your divine guidance and mercy.

*Your servants at the International Society for Cow Protection.*

## ISKCON Prison Ministry

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances.

As I was driving to work the other day I heard you say,

Bhīṣma was such a great personality. He is not ordinary man. Bhīṣma would not die unless he desires, “Now let me die.” Then he would die; otherwise not. So he was lying down on the deathbed, but was expecting to see Kṛṣṇa at the last stage. So therefore he was so great.

Your voice became choked up, and thinking of Kṛṣṇa’s affection for his devotees, you said, “And Kṛṣṇa was also going.” Unable to continue, you simply said, “Now you can chant Hare Kṛṣṇa.”

Tears filled my eyes and I pulled over, thinking of the painting in the Denver temple room of Bhīṣmadeva with the Pāṇḍavas and other great souls, and yes, Kṛṣṇa is there. Every day I stand before that painting and try to hear my *japa,* and I think of you.

I think how you are the *senapati-bhakta,* the great commanding general of ISKCON. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our Bhīṣmadeva, you are the kind father, the grandsire, *ācārya,* instructing guru for everyone.

I think of how you boldly and bravely preached in 1962 alone at the most obscure and remote *tīrtha* on earth: the Tihar prison in Delhi.

Before coming to America you had found large receptive audiences for your *Bhāgavata* message, and you wrote your Godbrother Purī Mahārāja:

Dear Sri Puri,

Yesterday evening I have come to Vrindaban for a flying visit and shall return to Delhi on Monday 4 / 6 / 62 to attend the Jayal meeting on Tuesday 5 / 6 / 62 morning. I am glad to see that my Thursday lectures have brought some changes in the mind of the young offenders. If the lectures are continued I am sure to turn these offenders into saintly characters. The means which I have adopted is spiritual and it works quickly than any material means. If you give me chance to speak to all the members of the Jayal, it is quite possible for me to turn them into ideal characters.

In my next lecture on Tuesday the 5 / 6 / 62 morning, I would request you also to be present and see it personally how the transcendental medicine is acting upon them. I am just trying to make spiritual method as much practical as any other material cures with greater and persisting effect.

If a parent loses a child, if that child goes missing—how great the anxiety of the parents! Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are returning the missing children to the original father, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

I cannot possibly understand how great Kṛṣṇa’s love is for you. You have taught us that there are gradations of everything, and the greatest servant is he who wakes up the sleeping children and brings them home.

The goal of the ISKCON Prison Ministry is to bring Kṛṣṇa consciousness to prisons around the world, to provide opportunities for inmates to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and to guide inmates in their spiritual life.

You began preaching to prison inmates forty years ago, and it is my duty and recreation to assist you in this endeavor. You are the *sparśa-maṇi,* the touchstone of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Wherever we go, distribute books, and fight on your behalf we are successful. Here are some letters from just a few of the hearts you have touched:

**Chanting and Loving**

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Thank you for the books you sent. I’m eating up the Gita.

My brother, it truly pains my heart that there are such a vast amount of people out there in this world that don’t know Kṛṣṇa. So I keep praying for them.

Still chanting 16 to 22 rounds a day and loving it. I’m slow. The meds I take slow me down, but I get up at 1:00AM so I have plenty of time. I don’t like sleeping that much for it takes me away from Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and who would want to be away from him for very long, not I!

Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Ken Bovard  
California State Prison

**I Have Never Forgotten**

You asked about my realizations so I will share it with you.

Way back a long time ago in 1966-67 I lived in New York and was a drug addict in the lower east side of New York. I used to go to 27-1Ave., to the little Hare Kṛṣṇa Center where Swami Prabhupāda used to hold chants. I had a lot of problems at that time drugs and all, but the people where always warm, loving, and kind to me, and I slept in the center many a night when it was cold and I had no place to stay. I always knew that Swami Prabhupāda was an enlightened being who could share life and space with people and make the light shine in them also.

Well that was then and now is now. Life has had its ups and downs, good time and bad. But I have never forgotten the man who taught me to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Bill Wilhelm  
Oklahoma State Prison

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we get hundreds of such letters throughout the month! And you are the inspiration to go on fighting. You are our Bhīṣmadeva.

**Lead Me to Battle**

Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You are my general. Lead me to battle. How could I have known you would recruit me into your guerrilla army of missionaries?

How could I have known you had chosen such an obscure vocation for me?

And yet—I came to you not because I had any good qualifications but because I was desperate. My only hope was that you’d accept me.

Unfortunately, I am totally inept.

My only qualification is that I have complete faith in you. Who could not? You are my hero and inspiration. Like a lion you attack all foolish adversaries.

True to your name, you are fearless and unfazed by any material circumstance. You are my general. I am your hapless foot soldier.

Lead me to battle. Just fighting for you is victory and I do nothing else well.

I’m not even a good soldier. My discipline is lax. My attention is distracted, and yet I somehow just want to go on fighting for you. I want to die on the battlefield with my boots on fighting for you.

You have blessed me with the desire and ability to fight. Your *tejas,* power, is your purity and absolute determination.

Your *śakti* defeats all material so-called philosophers, scientists, and religionists of all varieties. In the midst of battle—you reveal the sun of Kṛṣṇa, slashing all misconceptions and false arguments to pieces.

My leader, my benevolent father—please don’t kick me away. I have no other shelter than you. A soldier without a general is a pathetic thing.

In this world I have nothing but you. My Godbrothers, my preaching mission and *sādhana—*all gifts from you.

Please allow this crippled soldier to continue to march with you although I am lame.

Allow me to fight for you although I am weak.

Kindly embrace me and encourage me although I am fallen and dirty. I need your loving touch also.

Let me die seeing your face, for you are my general and I am your son, servant, and soldier.

Please—lead me to battle.

*The spiritual warriors of the ISKCON Prison Ministry.*  
(written by Candraśekhara dāsa)

## ISKCON Television (ITV)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In the flower-bearing spring of ’72  
You emerged from the jet-way in a sea of devotees.  
Standing in the back of the chanters and dancers,  
I got my first glimpse of you,  
Who would become my spiritual master.

For a moment, only two were in that crowded gate.  
I realized that I was the one who had gotten there late.  
You had returned from some other space and time  
To resume your tireless efforts  
To lift me out of my material comforts.

“He’s back!” I realized with a dawning remembrance.  
“I’ve got another chance to liberate myself from my selfish indulgence.”  
Tears burst from my eyes,  
I sobbed like a child,  
“My guru has arrived.”  
The rest is gravy.

You floated through the ocean of saffron and white.  
Then you sat where you were in everyone’s sight.  
A hush fell in the terminal as you raised your finger to speak:  
“You are not this body; you are spirit soul.  
Wake up from your sleep.”  
Then I only recall tears of relief.

Each person who was there remembers that moment.  
It pierced all our doubts and etched its way in our hearts.  
Though my tears have long since dried,  
The meaning has not faded.  
Each time I return  
I stop being jaded.

Thirty years have passed since that magical meeting,  
But your presence is as near to me as I hear your greeting.  
Please allow me to continue to meditate on those feelings  
So I can continue to be inspired by your merciful dealings.

This year ITV produced two new videos in our SPV Śrīla Prabhupāda series: “Chanting Japa with Śrīla Prabhupāda / Morning Meditations” and “Śrīla Prabhupāda in New Dwārkā.”

We also campaigned vigorously to stop the pirating of ITV videos by other devotees. By your mercy we have successfully resolved that issue with the active help of the devotees at the Bhaktivedanta Archives, the GBC, and the principal devotees who were doing the unauthorized duplication. By the cooperation of all the parties, an agreement was reached which put the problem to rest in that case. A benefit of the attention that was paid to this conflict was a systematic and economical access to the entire SPV series in the VCD format, with quality control and proper accountability. Now Your Divine Grace is available to anyone in the world who has a computer and / or a television with a VCD player.

ITV has also made a video / VCD / DVD on the nine forms of Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva found in Ahobilam. Hosted by Dhruva dāsa, son of Dharmātmā dāsa, and Divya-priyā devī, the “Search for Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva—Nava Forms Found in Ahobilam” was put together by Yadubara dāsa and Nṛsiṁhānanda dāsa, who teamed up to make the first comprehensive documentary on this remote pilgrimage site.

In all ways, ITV has benefited by your instruction that our love for you would be demonstrated by how well we cooperate together.

ITV is embarking on an ambitious plan to re-edit all of the SPV series of videos from the original color-corrected digital betacam originals to DVD. Finally, you will be presented in a stable format made from the highest quality possible, to be valued forevermore by the millions of living entities who may be fortunate, as I was, to have your *darśana*.

You are so kind to have given us the opportunity to serve you by dovetailing our talents, the actual meaning of real renunciation.

“Therefore one has to work for the satisfaction of Viṣṇu. Any other work done in the material world will be a cause of bondage. \[O\]ne has to work in Kṛṣṇa consciousness to satisfy Kṛṣṇa. This is the great art of doing work, and in the beginning this process requires very expert guidance. One should therefore act very diligently, under the expert guidance of a devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. ” \[*Bhagavad-gītā* 3.9, purport\]

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are that expert devotee. You can train us how to elevate ourselves to the transcendental loving service of the Lord. Please forever keep us engaged by your expert navigation so we don’t make you return to pluck us from the material ocean of nescience. I pray that the next jet-way I see you at is at the entrance to the transcendental airplane taking us to Kṛṣṇaloka.

*Your aspiring servants at ITV.*  
(written by Nṛsiṁhānanda dāsa)

## ISKCON Youth Ministry

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Hare Kṛṣṇa. Please accept our humble obeisances.

I am blessed with the opportunity to write this offering to you. I don’t feel very qualified to say anything to you, but I will simply express what is on my mind at this time and hope that you understand.

As a child who was in *gurukula* my whole life, a child who is now an adult, I see that the most important decision of our lives, to know our relationship with God, was given to us from birth. This is a wonderful gift: knowing God from a very young age and experiencing the sweetness of loving God. I am experiencing, though, that an inner conflict arises as I grow up, no longer a simple child. I question, “Did I choose this path to Kṛṣṇa, or am I just following it automatically because it’s familiar to me?” I must decide if I consciously want to choose the path to Kṛṣṇa as an adult, as my parents and other devotees in Kṛṣṇa consciousness had the chance to do. Many gurukulis, including myself, are in various stages of questioning, withdrawing from, or returning to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Some never have to leave at all. They accept Kṛṣṇa from the start and are secure in that faith. Some leave never to return. Some withdraw and return so much stronger and clearer in faith for their experience. I just hope that you understand and Kṛṣṇa understands where each of us is coming from.

I can say that no matter what stage I’m in, I’m grateful for the valuable tools you have given us. You have taught us what questions to ask: “Who am I? Who is God? What is my purpose here? How can I find happiness?” These questions are so wonderful that half of the battle is over. I know what to look for. It’s very comforting to have that knowledge, to have a starting place when there are doubts.

Please bless your family of grandchildren in all of our stages of spiritual growth. Please help us accept one another as we are today and empower us to help one another move forward in new and beautiful ways. Thank you.

Sincerely,

*Your grandchildren at the ISKCON Youth Ministry, Alachua, Florida, USA.*  
(written by Raghunandinī dāsī)

## Māyāpur Institute of Higher Education

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Lord Gaurāṅga analyzed our situation¾*bhuliyā rohile tumi avidyāra bhare:* we have forgotten the way of devotional service and fallen into ignorance. And He appeared (here in Śrīdhāma Māyāpur) to awaken us: *jīv jāgo, jīv jāgo, gauracānda bole*. Lord Gaurāṅga concluded¾*āmi binā bandhu āra ke āche tomāra*¾other than Myself, you have no friend in this world. (Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, *Aruṇodaya-kīrtana*)

However, without your contribution, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the advent of our divine friend would still be unknown to us. Thus you, Prabhupāda, are our greatest friend, for you are the instrument through which Lord Gaurāṅga’s calling is being broadcast all over the world.

Through book distribution, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you wake up the world by teaching the purpose of life. Fortunate souls who hear Lord Gaurāṅga’s calling are provided with support and training in your ISKCON temples worldwide. Your vision for Śrīdhāma Māyāpur includes a big center in Mayapur where this \[spiritual\] education will be internationally imparted. Students from all parts of the world will go there to take education in this important subject. \[Letter to Ātreya Ṛṣi dāsa, 20 August 1971\]

With the Māyāpur Institute of Higher Education we are endeavoring to establish such an institution. MIHE offers courses in śāstric study, Deity worship, administration, preaching and counseling skills, and other fields of devotional service. We want to assist you in teaching devotees “the way of devotional service” that we had forgotten. On this exceedingly important day celebrating your appearance, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are begging for your blessings in our attempt to assist you.

*Your aspiring servants at MIHE.*

## Padayātrā India

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our humble obeisances into the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your divine appearance!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said in a conversation in Vṛndāvana that we should introduce millions of bullock-cart parties for spreading the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu in the whole world. We can understand that you have such a great desire in your mind that each conditioned soul gets the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu has a desire to spread the holy name to every village and town, but you have a greater desire than even the Lord has. Pure devotees are more merciful than the Lord Himself. You want to help all the conditioned souls that have been suffering in this material world from time immemorial. Now, by your mercy, many people in the world are chanting the holy name of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Before departing to the eternal abode of Lord Kṛṣṇa, Goloka Vṛndāvana, you gave a divine instruction to your disciple Lokanāth Swami. Then the most wonderful preaching program started—Padayātrā India—guided by Lokanāth Swami. Since 1984 this program has been going on without a stop, completely by your mercy. Through sun, rain, and cold, day after day, month after month, and year after year, the Padayātrā continues. Lord Gaurāṅga and Lord Nityānanda, along with you and the Padayātrīs, have visited unlimited holy places, distributing your books, inspiring congregational devotees, and giving a chance to each and every person to chant the holy name of the Lord. In this way you have engaged so many people in the service of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Padayātrā preaching is very successful because you are personally present on the bullock cart. Every devotee feels that you are personally sitting on our cart and guiding us. Sometimes people try to speak with you. Sometimes people ask the Padayatrīs, “Why doesn’t your swami speak with us?”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the Padayātrā program is very healthy spiritually for everyone who has a desire to progress on the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and especially for *brahmacārīs* and *sannyāsīs*. It helps keep them pure and gives them a first-class chance to preach. Simple living and high thinking—this is Padayātrā.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the opportunity to serve Your Divine Grace and your devotees. We will observe the eighteenth anniversary of Padayātrā on Rādhāṣṭamī, and today we are observing your divine appearance day (Vyāsa-pūjā) here in Dvārakā-dhāma.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, of course you already have blessed us, no doubt, but please give us more blessings so that we can organize more such wonderful programs. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are thinking to organize a new Padayātrā in Maharashtra. So please bless us and help all those who are sincere to serve your mission and in this way bring all the conditioned souls we come across to the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara.

Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Our eternal obeisance to you.  
*Your servants on Padayātrā India.*

## Padma Academy

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

You have quoted, “The child is the father of the man,” and indeed our school has come full circle. Our principal and main teachers were born and raised in your ISKCON movement. They bring to the school not only academic expertise coupled with deep spiritual knowledge and realization. They also empathize with the needs and desires of these devotees in young bodies who are training to be Vaiṣṇavas while Western demonic influences surround them.

And among the students are the children of our gurukula graduates; we are now educating “the third generation.” Here we experience the continuity of what you established. Once, when asked if your movement would endure, you responded yes, because gurukula was training the next generation of Vaiṣṇavas and preachers.

How fortunate we are to be engaged in what you called the most important project! We pray that you grant us the wisdom, devotion, and practical expertise to manifest your desires for these children. May our schools become the ideal you described and envisioned.

*Your servants at Padma Academy, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA.*  
(written by Ūrmilā-devī dāsī)

## Prabhupāda Village

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you and your eternal service to Lord Kṛṣṇa!

Someone recently exclaimed that he thought devotees in our community and your movement in general were in some sort of suspended state waiting for a messiah to come and set everything on the right path again. As I pondered this view put forth by this prabhu, I thought of you and how you created this movement by the order of your spiritual master and by your determination to carry out that order. Many times you explained that you had success because you never added to or subtracted from the teachings of your spiritual master. When they told you that you would have to adopt Western ways, you disagreed because you didn’t come to the West to learn from us but to give us something that we needed. You didn’t adopt Western dress, you didn’t adopt the Western diet, and you openly criticized the Western idea of culture. You had no choice because you knew what Kṛṣṇa wants and what will make any living being truly happy and satisfied—a God-centered culture. You set the standard for transcendental existence in this material world by your example, and under the express direction of Lord Kṛṣṇa you gave us the manual for the next 10,000 years, your books. You set up an organization that enables anyone to get on the right path, the path back to Godhead, and you left it for all those who would come after you to at least maintain what you started.

The dictionary defines *messiah* as “one who is anticipated as, regarded as, or professes to be a savior or liberator.” Although you caught us completely by surprise, you certainly are our savior and our liberator. And just as your spiritual master is always present with you because you don’t deviate from his guidance, so you will always be present with those who adhere to your instructions. So when “the messiah” comes, how will we recognize him? The only way will be to see him in terms of your teachings. But if we all just wait for a “messiah” and never have the courage ourselves to step up to the plate, how will your movement continue? You encouraged us all and pushed us all toward the goal, creating a culture that will support Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the chanting of the Lord’s holy name. Arjuna had the option: he could stand and fight and be glorious, or he could avoid his duty and fade into oblivion. So we must push on as your representatives, submissive to your will, which is one with the Lord’s, and fight against illusion and doubt.

We pray that you give us the heart to carry on in the midst of all that goes on around us in this godless society, and that you give us the will to submit to you. Please give us the understanding that Arjuna had after Lord Kṛṣṇa removed his illusion.

My dear Kṛṣṇa, O infallible one, my illusion is now gone. I have regained my memory by Your mercy. I am now firm and free from doubt and am prepared to act according to Your instructions. \[*Bhagavad-gītā* 18.73\]

Make us go forward, armed with your instructions, and create an ideal society. We have here in our country community all the material ingredients. We just need the will to please you.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Hare Kṛṣṇa!

*Your servants at Prabhupāda Village, in Sandy Ridge, North Carolina, USA.*

## Prabhupāda-seva Rural Community

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

To understand the consciousness of a pure devotee is an impossible task. As you boarded the *Jaladuta,* ascending the gangplank, who among the stevedores, crew, or bystanders could have realized the importance of your presence? Even if they had realized you were traveling to America in a penniless state, could they ever have understood the importance of your mission or your courage to leave Vṛndāvana-dhāma and Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara for your soon-to-be digs in the Bowery? How could they ever have comprehended how these actions illustrated the depth of your faith and love for your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda, and Lord Kṛṣṇa? Could they ever have realized that one of the greatest moments in the history of mankind was occurring among the din and dinge of the port of Calcutta? Before their unknowing eyes, pure love of Godhead was overflowing the banks of India, and by your guidance and potency it would spread to every corner of the planet in just a few short years.

Years later, in 1976, with your *kṛṣṇa-prema* flowing throughout the globe in the form of transcendental literatures, you visited Toronto. Now decorated with thousands of disciples, you entered the temple room sporting your golden complexion and golden effulgence, causing many to gasp at their first sight of a *mahā-bhāgavata*. You stood among us, but how could we ever comprehend the depth of your love, the magnitude of your Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and the blissfulness of your intimate relationship with Kṛṣṇa? You have taught us what is real love. Not the “love” bantered about in novels or the cinema, but true love. A love which is selfless, ever-increasing, and everlasting. It is a love made real and tangible to us through your actions and writings, and it acts as our only inspiration today.

Time continued to pass, and the year 1977 found some of us chanting at your bedside. You were gravely ill, and as we sat there we agonized internally about your physical condition. Then, unexpectedly, you spoke, but because you were so weak your servant had to scramble to place his ear directly adjacent to your mouth to hear your words. You summoned a disciple who was overseeing the first “Life Comes from Life” conference and asked him about the accommodations for the soon-to-be visiting scientists. As we sat there lamenting the condition of your body, you were meditating on how to serve your beloved Lord Kṛṣṇa and continue to smash the myths and atheism of modern science. Never had your instructions about our not being our bodies seemed so clear and your position of *ācārya* so self-evident.

Now, many years later, we continue to be spiritually propelled by your example and instruction. We thank you for having stood among us even though we were so undeserving. We were lost, and you kindly turned us in the right direction by providing us the opportunity to engage in devotional service while you converted our wayward minds and senses with *bhāgavata* philosophy.

On such an auspicious day, we can only fall at your lotus feet and offer you our respects, gratitude, and prayer. Our prayer is that you please grant us the opportunity of serving you and Lord Kṛṣṇa eternally in any way you see fit. This would be our life’s perfection and greatest privilege. We thank you from the bottom of our hearts for what you have done for us. No words can suffice, but please accept this humble and inadequate attempt from your eternal servants.

*Your servants at the Prabhupāda-seva Rural Community, Campbellford, Ontario, Canada.*

## Radio Krishna Centrale

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare*  
*Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

You are without doubt a special personality, a perfect Vaiṣṇava. You forever change the lives of all those you directly or indirectly come in contact with. You have revealed the innermost thoughts of the Supreme Lord, and you have shown us who is a real, perfect, pure devotee of the Lord. You are His utmost dear servant. Nobody can be compared with Your Divine Grace. In you there is no confusion, no delusion, no illusion, no laziness, no weakness, no pride, no ignorance, no fear, no arrogance, no division, and no discouragement, but only unadulterated pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You said that there are no problems in Kṛṣṇa consciousness but that problems are there when Kṛṣṇa consciousness is missing, just as there is no darkness in the light but darkness is there where the light is missing.

\* \* \*

Dear Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa,

You are the Supreme Personality of Godhead and the supreme controller. Remembrance and forgetfulness are also under Your control. Please let us always remember Śrīla Prabhupāda and never forget His Divine Grace.

**A Prayer to Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Other memories wane while the seasons unfold,  
But my thinking of you never wilts or grows old.

Your divine lotus eyes, lotus hands, lotus feet,  
Your “Please bring back to Kṛṣṇa whoever you meet,”

Your deep wisdom and vision of fathomless length  
Are still here even now giving me hope and strength.

Still the sweet nectar pouring from your lotus mouth  
Fills my mind, and it’s all that my life is about.

I don’t want something else, something less, something “more.”  
All I want is to realize that you’re mine, ’cuz I’m yours.

You’re my master and lord, my well-wisher and friend,  
You’re my Śrī Prabhupād from beginning to end.

Please continue to guide us, we’ll stop every strife,  
We just want to serve and please you life after life.

Someone says it’s a test, someone says that you’ve gone,  
Someone, sad and confused, says we’re now all alone.

But my duty’s to serve and to pray you’ll accept  
This poor lost fallen soul as your son, as your pet.

And even if time seems to keep us apart,  
Forever you’ll live in the core of my heart.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, tie us tightly at your lotus feet and never let us go.

*Your humble servants at Radio Krishna Centrale, Terni, Italy.*  
(written by Tridaṇḍī dāsa)

## Rūpānuga Vedic College

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our *daṇḍavats* at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this most auspicious day of your appearance in this world, we beg you to accept our humble service, glorification, and prayers at your lotus feet. We are very thankful to you for what you have done for us.

There are many Vaiṣṇavas, but not many of them are willing to preach Kṛṣṇa’s glories and convert others, especially on such a level as Your Divine Grace did. Kṛṣṇa created the whole material world just so that living entities may regain their original appreciation of Him. And Kṛṣṇa personally says:

For one who explains this supreme secret to the devotees, pure devotional service is guaranteed, and at the end he will come back to Me. There is no servant in this world more dear to Me than he, nor will there ever be one more dear. \[*Bhagavad-gītā* 18.68–69\]

You gave us not just knowledge but a shelter where we can avoid all materialistic association, all the dirt of this world. By your unlimited mercy we have this wonderful Society. In this Society we can surrender to Kṛṣṇa without limit and just serve Him. It is so beautiful just to serve Kṛṣṇa without expectation of anything in return.

Also, we have unlimited association with your disciples, who perceive your transcendental qualities.

You gave us a transcendental *brahmacārī-āśrama,* were we need not worry about our maintenance because authorities are taking care of our needs. Therefore we can be free from feelings of any kind of possessiveness. This is exactly in accordance with Kṛṣṇa’s instructions for a transcendentalist in *Bhagavad-gītā* 6.10. And this is real freedom.

In other words, you gave us everything we need to become saintly persons in this world.

Now please bless us that we will be able to maintain your program as it is and thus attain perfection in spiritual life.

*Your humble servants at Rūpānuga Vedic College, Kansas City, Kansas, USA.*  
(written by Urukrama dāsa)

## Saraswati Charitable Trust

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your ever-present transcendental love and inspiration!

When we enter into one of your transcendental temples, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and stand before the Deities, there is an immediate sense of relief. Relief from material worries, disappointments, and frustrations. Relief from heartaches and painful distress of all kinds. This immediate sense of relief enters into us through all our senses, and then pervades the deepest parts of our body, mind, intelligence, and soul. Transcendental peace and happiness pervade us like brilliant morning sunshine pervading a cloudless sky. You freely brought us this Vaikuṇṭha atmosphere directly from the spiritual world, and you delivered it right into our hearts. You brought us an eternal gift that is beyond all description and beyond all measure.

By your grace and inspiration, this past year we were able to provide the funding for the first-ever printing of your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* in the Urdu language, the national language of Pakistan. Pakistan is surely an epicenter of great material suffering as I write these words. Yet, just as the Vaikuṇṭha atmosphere of your temples brings direct and great relief from all material suffering, so your transcendental literatures, made so much sweeter by your divine touch, provide that very same solace, hope, and transcendental peace to all the people of the world who are fortunate enough to see, touch, and read them.

Indeed, the reports are coming in that your Urdu *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* is being received with overwhelming appreciation by the scholars and residents of that war-torn land. They, too, are tasting your gift of the Vaikuṇṭha atmosphere and are becoming deeply and wonderfully affected.

Please continue to give us your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so we can be further inspired to help others taste your divine spiritual blessings. Please pour down your causeless mercy so we can ultimately leave behind this temporary place and enter into the inconceivably sweet atmosphere of our eternal home. By your mercy alone, we will meet you again there, and we will run to the waiting arms of our loving, smiling Lord.

*Your eternal servants at The Saraswati Charitable Trust.*  
(written by Satyanārāyaṇa dāsa)

## Śrī Māyāpur Project Development Committee

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Prabhupāda’s Son**

When you confront Arjuna’s situation,  
Unsure if you should fight or just pretend,  
And, thinking of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s affirmations,  
You toss out any thought of giving in;

If you can do your duty when it pains you,  
Without a hope of hearing “Job well done!”  
If you accept the fact that some out-gain you,  
Without a jealous thought toward anyone;

If in this ugly, brutal world you’re able  
To offer something lovely to the Lord,  
While keeping bread upon your family’s table  
And making service done its own reward;

If you meet all your worldly obligations  
And reap the crop you broke your back to plant,  
Aspiring for the highest destination,  
Expecting some will tell you that you can’t;

If you can take the blows inflicted on you,  
As minimized reactions to your sin,  
And turn your pain to strength for serving guru,  
Determined not to leave the Lord again;

If you no longer crave the worldly tempest,  
Reported in the paper and TV,  
And pick up *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* with interest,  
And feel yourself quite fortunate and free;

If you can keep a gentleman’s demeanor  
When every circumstance conspires to rile,  
And, thinking of the Lord within, remember  
That every spirit soul deserves a smile;

If you no longer think of sex as pleasure,  
As everybody does when they are young,  
And find yourself contented with the treasure  
Of Kṛṣṇa’s holy names upon your tongue;

If you can chant your rounds when some around you  
Are setting down their beads and blaming you,  
If you can keep your vows when Māyā hounds you,  
Ignoring her advice to chew the chewed;

If you encounter faults among devotees,  
Some weaknesses in those you thought were strong,  
And sit with them to share some dahl and *rotis*,  
And realize not all they do is wrong;

If your own faults and follies overcome you,  
Obscuring those in others you may see,  
If true appreciation flows out from you,  
Each time you greet another devotee;

If you can be straightforward in your dealings,  
And shed the taste for gossip, hate, and lies,  
And work in such a way to foster healing,  
Without thinking yourself too good or wise;

If you trust those of greater realization  
To guide your own devotion to the Lord,  
Make friends with those who share your situation,  
And kindly help the novices move forward;

If you can set aside your own conceptions,  
And take Gaurāṅga’s order on your head,  
And help one soul forsake this world’s deceptions  
And take to Kṛṣṇa consciousness instead;

If you chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, loud and often,  
And fill your days with loads of service done,  
The gatekeepers of Goloka Vṛndāvana  
Will welcome you—you’re Prabhupāda’s son.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants at Śrī Māyāpur Project Development Committee.*  
(written by Kalakaṇṭha dāsa)

## Vṛndāvana Institute of Higher Education

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

You said that by testing a grain of rice one can see if the whole pot is sufficiently cooked. To glorify your compassionate spreading of Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, I would like to tell you about the death of my mother, one “grain of rice” who came by chance into your association.

My mother, a very ordinary conditioned soul, first came in contact with you when she found out I was interested in becoming a devotee. Her reaction: she contacted a group of deprogrammers. Only later did she tell me that they wanted too much money, and her Jewish frugality won out—she didn’t hire them.

We didn’t speak for nearly a year. Then my mother threatened to commit suicide if I took up Kṛṣṇa consciousness. A merciful devotee encouraged me to remain determined: “Don’t worry. She will quit her body remembering Kṛṣṇa!”

Gradually Mom came to accept me as a member of ISKCON, and she even took a liking to some of your servants, such as Keśava Bhāratī Prabhu and Jaḍa Bhārata Prabhu. She visited your temples in Long Island and San Diego, but she was not interested in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. She couldn’t even pronounce the word “Kṛṣṇa” properly. She would say “Krushner.”

In her 70s, she gradually acquired Alzheimer’s disease and could not keep track of her life. Finally, with my family’s blessings, she came to Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma so my wife and I could take care of her. She took *prasādam* daily, regular heard your *bhajan* tapes, and associated only with devotees. After four months in Vraja, she got ill and left this world wearing *kaṇṭhi-mālā,* with Rādhā-kuṇḍa water in her mouth, and listening to you sing *Gurv-aṣṭakam*.

The thought of you boarding the *Jaladuta* has always inspired me. Without that supreme act of mercy and sacrifice, where would any of us be? Surely Enid Bloch would never have left her body hearing you glorify Lord Kṛṣṇa in Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma. Thank you for saving her.

*Your servants at the Vṛndāvana Institute of Higher Education.*  
(written by Braja Bihārī dāsa)
